   123→## Step 2: DESIGN - Palette & Font Plan
   124→
   125→**Before writing any ReportLab code, you MUST generate the color palette via the `palette.generate` command.** This is not optional. Do NOT hardcode hex colors. Do NOT pick colors by feel.
   126→
   127→### 2a. Generate Palette (MANDATORY)
   128→
   129→**Run this command FIRST. Copy-paste its output directly into your Python script:**
   130→
   131→```bash
   132→python3 "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/pdf.py" palette.cascade --title "<document title>" --mode minimal --format reportlab
   133→```
   134→
   135→> ⚠️ **Use `palette.cascade` (V2), NOT the old `palette.generate`.** Cascade palette outputs unified color roles for cover, body, charts, and tables from one base hue.
   136→
   137→The command auto-derives the design intent from the document title, computes a mathematically harmonious palette, and outputs ready-to-paste ReportLab Python code:
   138→
   139→```python
   140→# ━━ Cascade Palette (auto-generated by design_engine.py palette-cascade) ━━
   141→from reportlab.lib import colors
   142→
   143→# XL tier: backgrounds (area > 50%, S ≤ 0.08)
   144→PAGE_BG       = colors.HexColor('#f5f4f3')
   145→SECTION_BG    = colors.HexColor('#f0efed')
   146→
   147→# L tier: surfaces (area 20-50%, S ≤ 0.15)
   148→CARD_BG       = colors.HexColor('#eae8e4')
   149→TABLE_STRIPE  = colors.HexColor('#edebe8')
   150→
   151→# M tier: structural fills (area 5-20%, S ≤ 0.30)
   152→HEADER_FILL   = colors.HexColor('#3d5a6e')
   153→COVER_BLOCK   = colors.HexColor('#4a6575')
   154→
   155→# S tier: edges & icons (area 1-5%, S ≤ 0.50)
   156→BORDER        = colors.HexColor('#b8c0c7')
   157→ICON          = colors.HexColor('#4e6d7a')
   158→
   159→# XS tier: emphasis (area < 1%, S ≤ 0.75)
   160→ACCENT        = colors.HexColor('#2f97b9')
   161→ACCENT_2      = colors.HexColor('#3a8a7d')
   162→
   163→# Typography
   164→TEXT_PRIMARY  = colors.HexColor('#252422')
   165→TEXT_MUTED    = colors.HexColor('#8d8981')
   166→
   167→# Table colors (derived from cascade tiers — NOT from ACCENT)
   168→TABLE_HEADER_COLOR = HEADER_FILL          # M tier, low-sat — harmonious with body
   169→TABLE_HEADER_TEXT  = colors.white          # White text on dark header
   170→TABLE_ROW_EVEN     = colors.white          # Clean white
   171→TABLE_ROW_ODD      = TABLE_STRIPE          # L tier, very subtle stripe
   172→```
   173→
   174→**Options:**
   175→- `--mode minimal` (default, recommended for 50%+ of documents) | `dark` | `pastel` | `jewel` | `light`
   176→- `--harmony auto` (default, recommended) | `complementary` | `split_complementary` | `analogous` | `triadic` | `monochrome`
   177→- `--format reportlab` (recommended) | `json` | `css` | `summary`
   178→- `--seed <int>` - for reproducible palettes across regenerations
   179→
   180→**⚠️ FORBIDDEN:**
   181→- ❌ Writing `colors.HexColor('#xxxxxx')` with any hex value you chose yourself
   182→- ❌ Using `colors.red`, `colors.blue`, or any ReportLab named color for design elements
   183→- ❌ Skipping this step and picking colors "that look good"
   184→- ❌ Using different palettes for different sections of the same document
   185→- ❌ Using `ACCENT` (XS tier, high saturation) for table headers — use `HEADER_FILL` (M tier)
   186→- ❌ Using the old `palette.generate` — always use `palette.cascade`
   187→
   188→**✅ The ONLY acceptable way to get colors:** Run `palette.cascade`, copy the output.
   189→
   190→### 2b. Color Application Rules
   191→
   192→| Element | Color Source | Tier | Notes |
   193→|---------|-------------|------|-------|
   194→| Table headers | `HEADER_FILL` (bg) + white (text) | M | Low-sat, harmonious with body |
   195→| Table odd rows | `TABLE_STRIPE` | L | Very subtle same-hue stripe |
   196→| Table even rows | `colors.white` | — | Clean white |
   197→| Section titles | `HEADER_FILL` or `TEXT_PRIMARY` | M/text | Match heading hierarchy |
   198→| Body text | `TEXT_PRIMARY` | text | Never use pure #000000 |
   199→| Muted/meta text | `TEXT_MUTED` | text | Dates, captions, footnotes |
   200→| Horizontal rules | `BORDER` at 30% opacity | S | Thin, unobtrusive |
   201→| Chart colors | `ACCENT` → `ACCENT_2` → `HEADER_FILL` → `ICON` | XS/M/S | Series colors (see charts.md) |
   202→| Badges / tags | `ACCENT` | XS | Small area, high sat OK |
   203→| Card backgrounds | `CARD_BG` | L | Container fills |
   204→
   205→### 2c. Forbidden
   206→
   207→- ❌ Hardcoding hex colors like `#1F4E79`, `#555555`, `#888888` in code
   208→- ❌ Using `colors.red`, `colors.blue` or any ReportLab named color for design elements
   209→- ❌ Choosing colors by "feel" without running palette first
   210→- ❌ Using different accent colors across tables/sections in the same document
   211→
   212→> **Exception**: Semantic colors for data visualization (green for positive, red for negative) are acceptable but should be muted variants derived from the palette when possible.
   213→
   214→---
   215→
   216→## Step 3: EDIT - Content Transformation
   217→
   218→**Before writing any code, restructure raw text into visual roles.** This is the most impactful quality step - skipping it produces "Word document with a PDF extension".
   219→
   220→### 3a. Typographic Role Extraction
   221→
   222→Parse raw text and categorize content into visual roles:
   223→
   224→| Role | ReportLab Mapping | Description |
   225→|------|------------------|-------------|
   226→| **Hero / Display** | Cover title (36-42pt) or section opener | 1-5 words, the emotional hook |
   227→| **Kicker / Eyebrow** | Subtitle or section intro (10-12pt, muted) | Tiny context line above main heading |
   228→| **Data Sculpture** | CalloutBox or bold stat block | Extract impactful numbers (97%, $4.2M, -45ms) |
   229→| **Pull Quote** | BlockQuote (italic + left indent 24pt + accent border) | Most provocative sentence, standalone |
   230→| **Body** | Standard paragraph | Everything else |
   231→
   232→**Rule**: Before writing any body paragraph, scan for numbers with units. Every metric like "revenue grew by 12%" or "latency dropped to 45ms" MUST be extracted into a CalloutBox or metrics table - never buried in paragraph prose. See §Data-to-Ink Ratio Rules below.
   233→
   234→### 3b. Section Pacing
   235→
   236→- If any H1 section has **>400 words** of continuous body text without visual breaks, split into H2 subsections or insert a visual break (table, chart, callout)
   237→- Aim for at least 1 visual element (table/chart/callout) per 2-3 pages of body text
   238→- Dense text walls are the #1 sign of poor report design
   239→
   240→### 3c. Content Sanitization for External Sources (MANDATORY when source is PDF/OCR/web)
   241→
   242→When content is extracted from an external PDF, OCR output, or web scrape, it may contain invisible garbage characters that cause garbled rendering. **You MUST sanitize the text content before writing it into any `Paragraph()` or `canvas.drawString()`.**
   243→
   244→This is different from `code.sanitize` (which cleans Python source code). Content sanitization cleans the **text data itself**.
   245→
   246→```python
   247→import re
   248→
   249→def content_sanitize(text: str) -> str:
   250→    """Strip invisible/control chars from externally-sourced text."""
   251→    # Remove control chars (except \n \t)
   252→    text = re.sub(r'[\x00-\x08\x0b\x0c\x0e-\x1f\x7f]', '', text)
   253→    # Remove zero-width chars
   254→    text = re.sub(r'[\u200b-\u200f\u2028-\u202f\u2060\ufeff]', '', text)
   255→    # Remove replacement char (upstream encoding error)
   256→    text = text.replace('\ufffd', '')
   257→    # Remove variation selectors
   258→    text = re.sub(r'[\ufe00-\ufe0f]', '', text)
   259→    # Remove private use area chars
   260→    text = re.sub(r'[\ue000-\uf8ff]', '', text)
   261→    return text
   262→```
   263→
   264→**When to use:**
   265→| Source | Must sanitize? |
   266→|--------|---------------|
   267→| User typed text in prompt | No (usually clean) |
   268→| Extracted from PDF via `pdf.py extract.text` | **YES** |
   269→| OCR output | **YES** |
   270→| Web scrape / HTML strip | **YES** |
   271→| LLM-generated content | No (usually clean) |
   272→
   273→⚠️ **Do NOT confuse** `code.sanitize` (Step 6, cleans Python code) with `content_sanitize()` (Step 3, cleans text data). They solve different problems.
   274→
   275→---
   276→
   277→## Step 3.5: NUMBERING — 正文才是第一章 (MANDATORY)
   278→
   279→> ⚠️ **封面、目录、摘要、前言都不是章节。正文第一节 = 第一章/Chapter 1，永远。** Outline 的 `index` 是排序序号，不是章节号。混淆两者会导致文档从“第三章”或“第四章”开始。
   280→
   281→**写代码前必须输出编号映射表：**
   282→
   283→```
   284→| Outline Index | Type    | Chapter # | Title            |
   285→|---------------|---------|-----------|------------------|
   286→| 1             | cover   | —         | 封面               |
   287→| 2             | toc     | —         | 目录               |
   288→| 3             | content | 第一章    | 概要               |
   289→| 4             | content | 第二章    | 引言               |
   290→| ...           | ...     | ...       | ...              |
   291→```
   292→
   293→**铁律：**
   294→- cover/toc/back_cover/摘要/前言 → 无编号
   295→- 第一个 content 节点 = 第一章，永远从 1 开始
   296→- `page_title()` 必须用此表的章节号，不是 Outline `index`
   297→
   298→---
   299→
   300→---
   301→
   302→## Character Safety Rule (MANDATORY)
   303→
   304→Three rules for safe character handling in ReportLab PDFs:
   305→
   306→**a) Superscripts and subscripts**: Use `<super>`/`<sub>` tags, never raw Unicode superscript/subscript characters (e.g., `\u00b2`, `\u2082`).
   307→
   308→**b) Emoji**: ReportLab cannot render emoji. If content contains emoji, use Creative brief (HTML + Playwright).
   309→
   310→**c) Font fallback for mixed CJK/Latin text (MANDATORY)**: After registering fonts, you **MUST** call `install_font_fallback()` once. This automatically wraps missing-glyph characters in `<font>` tags inside every `Paragraph()`. No manual `<font name="...">` wrapping needed for mixed Chinese-English text. **Skipping this call is the #1 cause of Helvetica garbled text in CJK documents.**
   311→
   312→**d) canvas.drawString / drawRightString CJK font rule (MANDATORY)**: `install_font_fallback()` only works on `Paragraph()` objects. It does NOT affect `canvas.drawString()` / `canvas.drawRightString()` / `canvas.drawCentredString()`. If the text passed to any `canvas.draw*String()` call contains CJK characters (Chinese/Japanese/Korean), you **MUST** call `canvas.setFont('NotoSerifSC', size)` (or another registered CJK font) before drawing. Using FreeSerif / Helvetica / Times for CJK canvas text = garbled output.
   313→
   314→```python
   315→# ❌ WRONG — FreeSerif has no CJK glyphs, '科大讯飞' will be garbled
   316→canvas.setFont('FreeSerif-Italic', 8)
   317→canvas.drawRightString(x, y, 'SZSE: 002230  ·  科大讯飞')
   318→
   319→# ✅ CORRECT — use CJK font for mixed text
   320→canvas.setFont('NotoSerifSC', 8)
   321→canvas.drawRightString(x, y, 'SZSE: 002230  ·  科大讯飞')
   322→
   323→# ✅ ALSO CORRECT — pure English text can use FreeSerif
   324→canvas.setFont('FreeSerif-Italic', 8)
   325→canvas.drawString(x, y, 'Prepared by Strategy Advisory Team')
   326→```
   327→
   328→Mathematical/relational operators (×, ÷, ±, ≤, √, ∑, ≅, ∫, π, ∠, Δ, etc.) are safe to use as literal characters in `Paragraph()` - both Noto Sans SC and FreeSerif have these glyphs.
   329→
   330→| Need | Correct Method | Correct Example |
   331→|------|---------------|---------|
   332→| Superscript | `<super>` tag in `Paragraph()` | `Paragraph('10<super>2</super> × 10<super>3</super> = 10<super>5</super>', style)` |
   333→| Subscript | `<sub>` tag in `Paragraph()` | `Paragraph('H<sub>2</sub>O', style)` |
   334→| Bold | `<b>` tag in `Paragraph()` | `Paragraph('<b>Title</b>', style)` |
   335→| Math operators | Literal char in `Paragraph()` | `Paragraph('AB ⊥ AC, ∠A = 90°, and ΔABC ≅ ΔDCF', style)` |
   336→| Scientific notation | Combined tags in `Paragraph()` | `Paragraph('1.2 × 10<super>8</super> kg/m<super>3</super>', style)` |
   337→
   338→```python
   339→from reportlab.platypus import Paragraph
   340→from reportlab.lib.styles import ParagraphStyle
   341→from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_LEFT, TA_CENTER, TA_JUSTIFY
   342→
   343→body_style = ParagraphStyle(
   344→    name="ENBodyStyle",
   345→    fontName="FreeSerif",
   346→    fontSize=10.5,
   347→    leading=18,
   348→    alignment=TA_JUSTIFY,
   349→)
   350→
   351→# CJK Body (Chinese documents)
   352→cn_body_style = ParagraphStyle(
   353→    name="CNBodyStyle",
   354→    fontName="NotoSerifSC", fontSize=10.5, leading=18,
   355→    alignment=TA_LEFT,      # ← NEVER TA_JUSTIFY for CJK
   356→    wordWrap='CJK',         # ← MANDATORY for CJK
   357→    firstLineIndent=2*4.5*mm,
   358→)
   359→
   360→# Superscript: area unit
   361→Paragraph('Total area: 500 m<super>2</super>', body_style)
   362→
   363→# Subscript: chemical formula
   364→Paragraph('The reaction produces CO<sub>2</sub> and H<sub>2</sub>O', body_style)
   365→
   366→# Scientific notation
   367→Paragraph('Speed of light: 3.0 × 10<super>8</super> m/s', body_style)
   368→
   369→# Combined
   370→Paragraph('E<sub>k</sub> = mv<super>2</super>/2', body_style)
   371→
   372→# Bold heading
   373→Paragraph('<b>Chapter 1: Introduction</b>', header_style)
   374→
   375→# Math symbols
   376→Paragraph('When ∠ A = 90°, AB ⊥ AC and ΔABC ≅ ΔDEF', body_style)
   377→```
   378→
   379→**Pre-generation check - before writing ANY string, ask:**
   380→> "Does this string contain a character outside basic CJK or Mathematical/relational operators?"
   381→> If YES → it MUST be inside a `Paragraph()` with the appropriate tag.
   382→> If it is a superscript/subscript digit in raw unicode escape form → REPLACE with `<super>`/`<sub>` tag.
   383→
   384→**NEVER rely on post-generation scanning. Prevent at the point of writing.**
   385→
   386→**Encoding safety - before writing ANY content text:**
   387→> "Does this string contain Japanese kana (の, が, は etc.) or rare Unicode symbols?"
   388→> If YES → REPLACE with safe plain Chinese equivalents. Japanese kana (Hiragana U+3040-U+309F, Katakana U+30A0-U+30FF) frequently corrupt to U+FFFD (�) when code passes through LLM output, heredoc, or terminal encoding layers.
   389→> Common safe replacements: `活の真鲷`→`活缔真鲷`, `盐烤の鲭鱼`→`盐烤鲭鱼`, `烤の鸡串`→`炭烤鸡串`.
   390→> If the character is genuinely needed, verify it survives a full write→read round-trip with `open(file, encoding='utf-8')`.
   391→
   392→---
   393→
   394→## Font Setup (Guaranteed Success Method)
   395→
   396→### CRITICAL: Allowed Fonts Only
   397→**You MUST ONLY use the following registered fonts. Using ANY other font is STRICTLY FORBIDDEN.**
   398→
   399→| Font Name | Usage | Path |
   400→|-----------|-------|------|
   401→| `NotoSerifSC` | **Chinese body text (primary)** |  `/usr/share/fonts/truetype/noto-serif-sc/NotoSerifSC-Regular.ttf` |
   402→| `NotoSerifSC-Bold` | **Chinese headings (primary)** |  `/usr/share/fonts/truetype/noto-serif-sc/NotoSerifSC-Bold.ttf` |
   403→| `Noto Sans SC` | Chinese fallback / sans-serif | `/usr/share/fonts/truetype/chinese/NotoSansSC-Regular.ttf` |
   404→| `Noto Sans SC Bold` | Chinese headings (sans-serif) | `/usr/share/fonts/truetype/chinese/NotoSansSC-Bold.ttf` |
   405→| `SarasaMonoSC` | Chinese code blocks | `/usr/share/fonts/truetype/chinese/SarasaMonoSC-Regular.ttf` |
   406→| `FreeSerif` | English text, numbers, tables | `freefont/FreeSerif.ttf` |
   407→| `DejaVuSans` | Formulas, symbols, code | `/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSansMono.ttf` |
   408→
   409→**FORBIDDEN fonts (DO NOT USE):**
   410→- ❌ Arial, Arial-Bold, Arial-Italic
   411→- ❌ Helvetica, Helvetica-Bold, Helvetica-Oblique
   412→- ❌ Courier, Courier-Bold
   413→- ❌ Any font not listed in the table above
   414→
   415→> **Font file locations note:** On macOS, font paths may be under `~/.openclaw/workspace/fonts/` or `~/Library/Fonts/` instead of `/usr/share/fonts/`. The registration code below uses a single `FONT_DIR` variable — set it to the correct base path for your system. All font sub-paths (e.g. `truetype/noto-serif-sc/NotoSerifSC-Regular.ttf`) are appended to `FONT_DIR`.
   416→
   417→### Font Registration Template
   418→```python
   419→from reportlab.pdfbase import pdfmetrics
   420→from reportlab.pdfbase.ttfonts import TTFont
   421→from reportlab.pdfbase.pdfmetrics import registerFontFamily
   422→
   423→import platform
   424→_IS_MAC = platform.system() == 'Darwin'
   425→if _IS_MAC:
   426→    FONT_DIR = os.path.expanduser('~/.openclaw/workspace/fonts')
   427→else:  # Linux
   428→    FONT_DIR = '/usr/share/fonts'
   429→
   430→# Chinese fonts — Noto Serif SC (primary, serif, high quality)
   431→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('NotoSerifSC', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/noto-serif-sc/NotoSerifSC-Regular.ttf'))
   432→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('NotoSerifSC-Bold', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/noto-serif-sc/NotoSerifSC-Bold.ttf'))
   433→# Chinese fonts — fallback / sans-serif (static weight files, NOT variable font)
   434→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('Noto Sans SC', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/chinese/NotoSansSC-Regular.ttf'))
   435→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('Noto Sans SC Bold', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/chinese/NotoSansSC-Bold.ttf'))
   436→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('SarasaMonoSC', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/chinese/SarasaMonoSC-Regular.ttf'))
   437→
   438→# English fonts — FreeSerif (4 weights)
   439→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('FreeSerif', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/freefont/FreeSerif.ttf'))
   440→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('FreeSerif-Bold', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/freefont/FreeSerifBold.ttf'))
   441→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('FreeSerif-Italic', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/freefont/FreeSerifItalic.ttf'))
   442→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('FreeSerif-BoldItalic', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/freefont/FreeSerifBoldItalic.ttf'))
   443→
   444→# Symbol/Formula font
   445→pdfmetrics.registerFont(TTFont('DejaVuSans', f'{FONT_DIR}/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSansMono.ttf'))
   446→
   447→# CRITICAL: Register font families to enable <b>, <i>, <super>, <sub> tags
   448→registerFontFamily('NotoSerifSC', normal='NotoSerifSC', bold='NotoSerifSC-Bold')
   449→registerFontFamily('Noto Sans SC', normal='Noto Sans SC', bold='Noto Sans SC Bold')
   450→registerFontFamily('FreeSerif', normal='FreeSerif', bold='FreeSerif-Bold', italic='FreeSerif-Italic', boldItalic='FreeSerif-BoldItalic')
   451→registerFontFamily('DejaVuSans', normal='DejaVuSans', bold='DejaVuSans')
   452→```
   453→
   454→### Font Configuration by Document Type
   455→
   456→**For Chinese PDFs:**
   457→- Body text: `NotoSerifSC` (primary) — serif font with sharp, elegant strokes
   458→- Headings: `NotoSerifSC-Bold` (MUST use for Chinese headings) — same family, bolder weight for clear hierarchy
   459→- Code blocks: `SarasaMonoSC`
   460→- Formulas/symbols: `DejaVuSans`
   461→- **In tables: ALL Chinese content MUST use `NotoSerifSC`, numbers use `FreeSerif`**
   462→- Fallback: `Noto Sans SC` (if NotoSerifSC unavailable)
   463→
   464→**For English PDFs:**
   465→- Body text: `FreeSerif`
   466→- Headings: `FreeSerif` (MUST use for English headings)
   467→- Code blocks: `DejaVuSans`
   468→- **In tables: ALL English content and numbers MUST use `FreeSerif`**
   469→
   470→**For Mixed Chinese-English PDFs:**
   471→- Call `install_font_fallback()` once after registering fonts - it automatically wraps characters in `<font>` tags so you don't need to do it manually.
   472→- If you still want manual control, you can use `<font name='...'>` tags, but the automatic fallback handles most cases.
   473→
   474→```python
   475→import sys, os
   476→sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'scripts'))
   477→from pdf import install_font_fallback
   478→
   479→# 1. Register fonts (as above)
   480→# 2. Install fallback - one line, handles all mixed-language text
   481→install_font_fallback()
   482→
   483→# 3. Just write naturally - no manual <font> wrapping needed!
   484→en_style = ParagraphStyle(name="EN", fontName="FreeSerif", fontSize=10.5, leading=18)
   485→cn_style = ParagraphStyle(name="CN", fontName="NotoSerifSC", fontSize=10.5, leading=18, wordWrap='CJK')
   486→
   487→Paragraph('MySQL、PostgreSQL、Redis', en_style)           # ✅ CJK comma auto-fallback to NotoSerifSC
   488→Paragraph('Beijing (北京) has 21M people', en_style)       # ✅ CJK chars auto-fallback
   489→Paragraph('价格:¥2,200~5,800/月', en_style)               # ✅ all CJK chars handled
   490→Paragraph('《巴黎协定》签署于2015年', en_style)               # ✅ CJK book title marks handled
   491→```
   492→
   493→**How it works:** `install_font_fallback()` monkey-patches `Paragraph.__init__` to scan each character against the font's `charToGlyph` table. Characters missing from the base font are automatically wrapped in `<font name="FallbackFont">`. The fallback chain is: English fonts → NotoSerifSC (primary) or Noto Sans SC (legacy), Chinese fonts → FreeSerif. For aesthetic optimization, Cyrillic text in NotoSerifSC/Noto Sans SC is automatically routed to FreeSerif (serif looks better for Cyrillic).
   494→
   495→### Chinese Plot PNG Method
   496→```python
   497→import matplotlib
   498→import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
   499→matplotlib.font_manager.fontManager.addfont('/usr/share/fonts/truetype/chinese/NotoSansSC-Regular.ttf')
   500→matplotlib.font_manager.fontManager.addfont('/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf')
   501→# Noto Sans SC for Chinese, DejaVu Sans catches symbols Noto Sans SC lacks (²³♠ etc.)
   502→plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['Noto Sans SC', 'DejaVu Sans']
   503→plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False
   504→```
   505→
   506→### Available Font Paths
   507→Run `fc-list` to get more fonts. Font files are typically located under:
   508→- `/usr/share/fonts/truetype/chinese/`
   509→- `/usr/share/fonts/truetype/english/`
   510→- `/usr/share/fonts/`
   511→
   512→---
   513→
   514→## Layout & Spacing Control
   515→
   516→### Page Breaks
   517→
   518→Follow the document type strategy defined in SOUL.md Rule 1.
   519→
   520→**Structural breaks (always - MANDATORY):**
   521→- Between cover page and TOC - **cover must NEVER share a page with anything else**
   522→- Between TOC and main content
   523→- Between main content and back cover
   524→
   525→**Content breaks (by document type):**
   526→- **Default (all document types)**: ❗ **Absolutely NEVER force page breaks before H1/H2** — content flows naturally, do not insert `PageBreak()` before section headings. This is an iron rule.
   527→- Resume / contract / letter → No content page breaks
   528→- Short article → No content page breaks
   529→- **Exception 1**: Academic papers / textbooks / teaching plans → `PageBreak()` before each H1 is acceptable (only if user explicitly requests academic formatting)
   530→- **Exception 2**: User explicitly requests page breaks between chapters
   531→
   532→**Anti-tear (mandatory — but with height limit):**
   533→```python
   534→from reportlab.platypus import KeepTogether
   535→from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
   536→from reportlab.lib.units import inch
   537→
   538→# Maximum height for KeepTogether blocks: 40% of page height
   539→# Blocks taller than this should NOT be kept together — they cause
   540→# the preceding page to be mostly empty when the block gets pushed down.
   541→MAX_KEEP_HEIGHT = A4[1] * 0.4  # ~336pt ≈ 12cm
   542→
   543→def safe_keep_together(elements):
   544→    """Wrap elements in KeepTogether only if their total height is reasonable.
   545→    If too tall, keep only the FIRST TWO elements together (e.g. title + table/image header)
   546→    to prevent title-content separation, while letting the rest paginate naturally.
   547→    """
   548→    total_h = 0
   549→    for el in elements:
   550→        w, h = el.wrap(A4[0] - 2*inch, A4[1])
   551→        total_h += h
   552→    if total_h <= MAX_KEEP_HEIGHT:
   553→        return [KeepTogether(elements)]
   554→    elif len(elements) >= 2:
   555→        # Keep at least title + first element together to prevent title orphaning
   556→        return [KeepTogether(elements[:2])] + list(elements[2:])
   557→    else:
   558→        return list(elements)
   559→
   560→# Heading stays with first paragraph
   561→story.extend(safe_keep_together([
   562→    heading_paragraph,
   563→    first_body_paragraph,
   564→]))
   565→
   566→# Image + caption: keep together ONLY if image is small
   567→story.extend(safe_keep_together([image, caption_paragraph]))
   568→
   569→# Table title + table: keep together ONLY if table is short
   570→if len(table_data) <= 15:
   571→    story.extend(safe_keep_together([table_title, table]))
   572→else:
   573→    # Long table: just keep title with the table start
   574→    story.extend(safe_keep_together([table_title]))
   575→    story.append(table)
   576→```
   577→
   578→**⚠️ KEY INSIGHT:** `KeepTogether` is the #1 cause of “empty page with chart on next page”.
   579→When a `KeepTogether` block is taller than the remaining space on the current page,
   580→ReportLab pushes the **entire block** to the next page, leaving the current page mostly empty.
   581→**Always use `safe_keep_together()` to cap the maximum block height.**
   582→
   583→**⚠️ Note:** Do NOT use `KeepWithNext` - it is unreliable in ReportLab 4.x.
   584→
   585→### Vertical Spacing Standards
   586→* Before tables: `Spacer(1, 18)` after preceding text
   587→* After tables: `Spacer(1, 6)` before table caption
   588→* After table captions: `Spacer(1, 18)` before next content
   589→* Between paragraphs: `Spacer(1, 12)` (~1 line)
   590→* Between H3 subsections: `Spacer(1, 12)`
   591→* Between H2 sections: `Spacer(1, 18)` (~1.5 lines)
   592→* Between H1 sections: `Spacer(1, 24)` (~2 lines)
   593→* NEVER use `Spacer(1, X)` where X > 24, except for H1 major breaks or cover page elements
   594→
   595→### Cover Page: HTML/Playwright Unified Cover System
   596→
   597→**⚠️ Report route covers are ALWAYS generated via HTML/Playwright**, using the same 5-template system defined in `typesetting/cover.md`. This ensures visual consistency across all routes (Report, Creative, Academic) and avoids the limitations of ReportLab for complex cover layouts.
   598→
   599→**Pipeline (Report route):**
   600→1. Generate **body PDF** via ReportLab (start with TOC or content - **no cover in story[]**)  
   601→2. Generate **cover HTML** following `typesetting/cover.md` 5-template system  
   602→3. **Run `poster_validate.py check-html` on cover HTML** — fix any ERRORs before rendering (overflow:hidden, font fallback, etc.)  
   603→4. **Run `cover_validate.js` on cover HTML** — detects text-vs-decorative-line overlaps. Non-zero exit = must fix before proceeding.
   604→   ```bash
   605→   node "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/cover_validate.js" cover.html
   606→   ```
   607→5. Render cover HTML → single-page PDF via Playwright (`html2poster.js`) — **NOT `html2pdf-next.js`** (which converts absolute→static and destroys cover layout). **⚠️ MUST pass `--width 794px` for A4 documents** (A4 = 210mm = 794px @96dpi). Without this, `html2poster.js` defaults to 720px (=540pt), which is narrower than A4 (595pt) and causes page size mismatch after merging with the body PDF.  
   608→6. **Merge: insert cover as page 0** of body PDF using pypdf → output single final PDF
   609→
   610→> **Why not ReportLab covers?** ReportLab is excellent for structured content (tables, paragraphs, flowables) but painful for visual design (geometric accents, precise absolute positioning, web fonts). HTML/CSS handles these natively. One cover system, one visual standard, zero inconsistency.
   611→
   612→**⚠️ Cover page is DEFAULT ON for all Report-route documents ≥ 3 pages.**
   613→
   614→The Report route handles formal, structured documents. Readers expect a professional cover. **Always generate a cover unless the document type is explicitly excluded below.**
   615→
   616→**ALWAYS add a cover page (default behavior):**
   617→- Research reports, experiment reports, lab reports, analysis reports
   618→- White papers, industry analysis, market research
   619→- Business proposals, project plans, feasibility studies
   620→- Technical documentation, product manuals, user guides
   621→- Annual reports, financial summaries, quarterly reviews
   622→- Any formal document ≥ 3 pages that will be shared externally or submitted
   623→
   624→**NEVER add a cover page (explicit exclusions only):**
   625→- **Resumes / CVs** - recruiters want content immediately
   626→- **Letters / memos** - single-page or short-form
   627→- **Forms / checklists / invoices** - functional documents
   628→- **Internal notes / meeting minutes** - brevity over presentation
   629→- **Documents ≤ 2 pages** - cover would be disproportionate
   630→- **User explicitly said "no cover"**
   631→
   632→**Rule of thumb:** If it's a report, analysis, proposal, or any formal document ≥ 3 pages → **add a cover**. When in doubt, add the cover. It's easier to remove a cover the user didn't want than to miss one they expected.
   633→
   634→#### Step 1: Generate Body PDF (ReportLab, no cover)
   635→
   636→Build the ReportLab document starting directly with TOC or first section. **Do NOT include any cover page in the `story[]` list.**
   637→
   638→```python
   639→# Body PDF - starts with TOC or first section, NO cover
   640→story = []
   641→
   642→# ⚠️ If document has TOC: use TocDocTemplate + multiBuild (see TOC Gate above)
   643→# If no TOC: use SimpleDocTemplate + build
   644→
   645→# WITH TOC (most reports):
   646→# doc = TocDocTemplate(output, pagesize=A4, ...)
   647→# story.append(toc)
   648→# story.append(PageBreak())
   649→# story.append(first_section_content)
   650→# doc.multiBuild(story)
   651→
   652→# WITHOUT TOC:
   653→# doc = SimpleDocTemplate(output, pagesize=A4, ...)
   654→# story.append(first_section_content)
   655→# doc.build(story)
   656→```
   657→
   658→#### Step 2: Generate Cover PDF via html2poster.js
   659→
   660→```bash
   661→# ALWAYS use html2poster.js for cover rendering (NOT html2pdf-next.js)
   662→# Cover pages use position:absolute layout — html2pdf-next.js pre-render hooks
   663→# convert absolute→static and destroy the layout. html2poster.js preserves it.
   664→node "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/html2poster.js" cover.html --output cover.pdf --width 794px
   665→```
   666→
   667→Or from Python:
   668→```python
   669→import subprocess, os
   670→
   671→def render_cover(html_path, pdf_path):
   672→    """Render HTML cover to PDF via html2poster.js.
   673→    
   674→    ⚠️ ALWAYS use html2poster.js for covers (NOT html2pdf-next.js).
   675→    Cover HTML uses position:absolute for layout. html2pdf-next.js pre-render
   676→    hooks convert absolute→static to prevent multi-page overlap, which
   677→    destroys cover layouts. html2poster.js preserves absolute positioning.
   678→    """
   679→    scripts_dir = os.path.expanduser('~/.openclaw/workspace/skills/pdf/scripts')
   680→    subprocess.run([
   681→        'node', os.path.join(scripts_dir, 'html2poster.js'),
   682→        html_path, '--output', pdf_path,
   683→        '--width', '794px',
   684→    ], check=True)
   685→```
   686→
   687→**Insertion script (single output PDF):**
   688→```python
   689→from pypdf import PdfReader, PdfWriter
   690→
   691→A4_W, A4_H = 595.28, 841.89  # A4 in points
   692→
   693→def normalize_page_to_a4(page):
   694→    """Scale a page to A4 if its dimensions don't match."""
   695→    box = page.mediabox
   696→    w, h = float(box.width), float(box.height)
   697→    if abs(w - A4_W) > 2 or abs(h - A4_H) > 2:
   698→        page.scale_to(A4_W, A4_H)
   699→    return page
   700→
   701→def insert_cover(cover_pdf, body_pdf, output_pdf):
   702→    """Insert cover as first page of body PDF → single output file."""
   703→    writer = PdfWriter()
   704→    # Cover as page 1
   705→    cover_page = PdfReader(cover_pdf).pages[0]
   706→    writer.add_page(normalize_page_to_a4(cover_page))
   707→    # Body pages follow
   708→    for page in PdfReader(body_pdf).pages:
   709→        writer.add_page(normalize_page_to_a4(page))
   710→    writer.add_metadata({'/Title': 'Report Title', '/Author': 'Z.ai', '/Creator': 'Z.ai'})
   711→    with open(output_pdf, 'wb') as f:
   712→        writer.write(f)
   713→```
   714→
   715→> **⚠️ Size pitfall:** Playwright `page.pdf(width='794px', height='1123px')` output PDF dimensions may differ from A4 (595.28×841.89pt) by 1-2pt. **Do not use PIL Image.save('x.pdf')** for PNG→PDF conversion — DPI mapping causes severe size errors. You must call `normalize_page_to_a4()` to unify dimensions before insertion.
   716→
   717→**Cover HTML template reference**: See `typesetting/cover.md` for the complete 5-Layout System with variants, typography scale, and bounding box rules.
   718→
   719→**→ MUST READ: `typesetting/cover.md`** — read the complete 5-Layout System with variants, typography scale, and bounding box rules before writing any cover HTML.
   720→
   721→**Cover design rules (summary - see cover.md for details):**
   722→- Page size: `width: 794px; height: 1123px` (A4 at 96dpi)
   723→- `body { margin: 0; padding: 0; overflow: hidden; }` - REQUIRED to avoid white borders
   724→- Load Google Fonts via `<link href="..." rel="stylesheet">` in `<head>` (NOT `@import url(...)` in CSS — `@import` must be first rule or is silently ignored) — Playwright fetches them at render time
   725→- **Background must be white or very light** (`#fff` / `#fafafa` / `#f5f8fb`), no dark solid backgrounds or gradient backgrounds
   726→- Primary color used only for text, lines, geometric accents - never as large-area background
   727→- Sophistication = whitespace + typography + restrained geometric accents
   728→
   729→**Cover Constitution (5-Layout System):**
   730→- **Pick a layout (01/03/04/06/07)** from `typesetting/cover.md` that matches the document tone. No global default - every selection must be a deliberate design decision.
   731→- **Maximum 4 components** on any cover. Typical recipe: Title + subtitle + 1 geometric accent + metadata.
   732→- **Typography Scale**: Title ≈ 45pt, Subtitle ≈ 25pt, Meta ≥ 18pt (never below 14pt). Tiny text = FAIL.
   733→- **Background layer (optional)**: See `typesetting/cover-backgrounds.md` for 3 recipes (A=极简弧线, B=工程十字轴+立柱, C=锐角切割+出血文字). Background renders BELOW all foreground content at 2-5% opacity.
   734→- **Mandatory `<br>` chunking**: Title MUST break every 2-4 words (CJK) or 3-5 words (English). Single-line title = FAIL.
   735→- **Bounding Box spatial dispersion**: Group elements into 2-3 bounding boxes at opposite regions (e.g., top-left + bottom-right). Never cluster everything into middle 40%.
   736→- **Safe Zone**: 12% top/bottom, 14% left/right padding on cover pages.
   737→- **No body text on cover**: Cover = title + subtitle + date + optional geometric decoration. All content goes to page 2+.
   738→- **Cover page isolation**: Cover NEVER shares a page with TOC or body content. In Report route this is inherent (separate PDFs merged via pypdf). If output shows cover + TOC on same page = **critical bug**, regenerate immediately.
   739→
   740→### Alignment and Typography
   741→- **CJK body**: Use `TA_LEFT` + 2-char indent. Headings: no indent.
   742→- **Font sizes**: Body 11pt, subheadings 14pt, headings 18-20pt
   743→- **Line height**: 1.5-1.6 (leading at **1.4x font size minimum**, recommended 1.5x for CJK)
   744→  - CJK characters are taller than Latin - 1.2x causes cramped lines
   745→  - Quick reference: 10pt→14pt min/18pt rec; 14pt→20pt min/22pt rec; 36pt→50pt min/54pt rec
   746→- ** Prohibited: fixed `rowHeights` in Table()**. Use `TOPPADDING` / `BOTTOMPADDING` to control row spacing. Fixed rowHeights cause content overflow clipping - the text renders but gets invisibly cut off.
   747→- **CRITICAL: Alignment Selection Rule**:
   748→  - Use `TA_JUSTIFY` only when ALL of:
   749→    * Text is predominantly English (≥ 90%)
   750→    * Column width is sufficiently wide (A4 single-column body)
   751→    * Font: Western fonts (FreeSerif)
   752→    * Chinese content: None or negligible
   753→  - Otherwise, always default to `TA_LEFT`
   754→  - For Chinese text, always add `wordWrap='CJK'` to ParagraphStyle
   755→
   756→### Style Configuration
   757→* Normal paragraph: `spaceBefore=0`, `spaceAfter=6-12`
   758→* Headings: `spaceBefore=12-18`, `spaceAfter=6-12`
   759→* **Headings must be bold**: Use `<b></b>` tags in Paragraph
   760→* Table captions: `spaceBefore=3`, `spaceAfter=6`, `alignment=TA_CENTER`
   761→* **CRITICAL**: For Chinese text, always add `wordWrap='CJK'` to ParagraphStyle
   762→
   763→---
   764→
   765→## Table Formatting
   766→
   767→### Standard Table Color Scheme (MUST USE for ALL tables)
   768→
   769→> **Colors MUST come from the `palette.cascade` output (Step 2).** The values below are variable names - they are populated by the cascade palette, never hardcoded.
   770→
   771→```python
   772→# Colors from palette.cascade (Step 2) — NEVER hardcode hex values
   773→# Table header uses HEADER_FILL (M tier, S ≤ 0.30) — NOT ACCENT (XS tier, too vivid)
   774→TABLE_HEADER_COLOR = HEADER_FILL          # M tier — low-sat, blends with document body
   775→TABLE_HEADER_TEXT  = colors.white          # White text for header (fixed)
   776→TABLE_ROW_EVEN     = colors.white          # White for even rows
   777→TABLE_ROW_ODD      = TABLE_STRIPE          # L tier — very subtle same-hue stripe
   778→```
   779→
   780→> **⚠️ Why NOT `ACCENT` for table headers?** `ACCENT` is XS tier (S = 0.50-0.75), designed for tiny elements like badges and tags. Table headers occupy 5-20% of visual area (M tier). Using high-saturation `ACCENT` on table headers creates jarring color blocks that clash with the low-saturation body. `HEADER_FILL` (S ≤ 0.30) is specifically designed for this area ratio.
   781→
   782→### Table Rules
   783→- Caption must be centered, added immediately after the table
   784→- Entire table must be centered on the page
   785→- **Header Row**: Dark background (from palette `HEADER_FILL`, M tier), white bold text
   786→- **Cell Margins**: Left/Right at least 120-200 twips
   787→- **Alignment**: Each body element within the same table must be aligned the same method
   788→- **Color consistency**: All tables in one PDF must use the same `HEADER_FILL` + `TABLE_STRIPE` scheme
   789→- **Spacing**: `Spacer(1, 18)` → Table → `Spacer(1, 6)` → Caption → `Spacer(1, 18)`
   790→
   791→### Table Centering Enforcement (MANDATORY)
   792→
   793→**Every table MUST be horizontally centered on the page. No exceptions.**
   794→
   795→```python
   796→# ReportLab: ALWAYS set hAlign='CENTER'
   797→table = Table(data, colWidths=col_widths, hAlign='CENTER')
   798→
   799→# Width rule: table total width should be 85-100% of available content width
   800→available_width = doc.width  # or page_width - left_margin - right_margin
   801→table_width = sum(col_widths)
   802→if table_width < available_width * 0.85:
   803→    # Scale up columns proportionally to fill space
   804→    scale = (available_width * 0.90) / table_width
   805→    col_widths = [w * scale for w in col_widths]
   806→
   807→# ❌ WRONG — no hAlign (defaults to LEFT, table drifts left)
   808→table = Table(data, colWidths=[100, 200, 150])
   809→
   810→# ✅ RIGHT — explicit centering
   811→table = Table(data, colWidths=[100, 200, 150], hAlign='CENTER')
   812→```
   813→
   814→**LaTeX equivalent:**
   815→```latex
   816→\begin{table}[htbp]
   817→  \centering  % MANDATORY
   818→  \begin{tabular}{...}
   819→  ...
   820→  \end{tabular}
   821→\end{table}
   822→```
   823→
   824→### Table Cell Content Rule (MANDATORY - NON-NEGOTIABLE)
   825→
   826→**ALL text content in table cells MUST be wrapped in `Paragraph()`. NO EXCEPTIONS.**
   827→
   828→❌ **PROHIBITED** - Plain strings:
   829→```python
   830→data = [
   831→    ['<b>Header</b>', 'Value'],           # Bold won't render!
   832→    ['Pressure', '1.01 × 10<super>5</super>'],  # Superscript won't work!
   833→]
   834→```
   835→
   836→✅ **REQUIRED** - All text in Paragraph:
   837→```python
   838→data = [
   839→    [Paragraph('<b>Header</b>', header_style), Paragraph('Value', header_style)],
   840→    [Paragraph('Pressure', cell_style), Paragraph('1.01 × 10<super>5</super>', cell_style)],
   841→]
   842→```
   843→
   844→**The ONLY exception**: `Image()` objects can be placed directly in table cells.
   845→
   846→### Units with Exponents (CRITICAL)
   847→- PROHIBITED: `W/m2`, `kg/m3`, `m/s2` (plain text exponents)
   848→- RIGHT: `Paragraph('W/m<super>2</super>', style)`, `Paragraph('kg/m<super>3</super>', style)`
   849→
   850→### Numeric Values in Tables (CRITICAL)
   851→- Large numbers MUST use scientific notation: `Paragraph('-1.246 × 10<super>8</super>', style)` not `-124600000`
   852→- Small decimals MUST use scientific notation: `Paragraph('2.5 × 10<super>-3</super>', style)` not `0.0025`
   853→- Threshold: Use scientific notation when |value| ≥ 10000 or |value| ≤ 0.001
   854→
   855→### Complete Table Example
   856→```python
   857→from reportlab.platypus import Table, TableStyle, Paragraph, Image
   858→from reportlab.lib.styles import ParagraphStyle
   859→from reportlab.lib import colors
   860→from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_CENTER, TA_LEFT, TA_RIGHT, TA_JUSTIFY
   861→
   862→header_style = ParagraphStyle(
   863→    name='TableHeader', fontName='FreeSerif', fontSize=11,
   864→    textColor=colors.white, alignment=TA_CENTER
   865→)
   866→cell_style = ParagraphStyle(
   867→    name='TableCell', fontName='FreeSerif', fontSize=10,
   868→    textColor=colors.black, alignment=TA_CENTER
   869→)
   870→
   871→data = [
   872→    [Paragraph('<b>Parameter</b>', header_style),
   873→     Paragraph('<b>Unit</b>', header_style),
   874→     Paragraph('<b>Value</b>', header_style)],
   875→    [Paragraph('Temperature', cell_style),
   876→     Paragraph('°C', cell_style),
   877→     Paragraph('25.5', cell_style)],
   878→    [Paragraph('Pressure', cell_style),
   879→     Paragraph('Pa', cell_style),
   880→     Paragraph('1.01 × 10<super>5</super>', cell_style)],
   881→    [Paragraph('Density', cell_style),
   882→     Paragraph('kg/m<super>3</super>', cell_style),
   883→     Paragraph('1.225', cell_style)],
   884→]
   885→
   886→table = Table(data, colWidths=[120, 80, 100])
   887→# ⚠️ Above uses hardcoded widths - OK for this 3-col example (120+80+100=300 < available ~460pt).
   888→# For real tables, ALWAYS calculate from available_width. See "Table Width Management" below.
   889→table.setStyle(TableStyle([
   890→    ('BACKGROUND', (0, 0), (-1, 0), ACCENT),           # Header: palette accent
   891→    ('TEXTCOLOR', (0, 0), (-1, 0), colors.white),
   892→    ('BACKGROUND', (0, 1), (-1, 1), colors.white),
   893→    ('BACKGROUND', (0, 2), (-1, 2), BG_SURFACE),       # Odd row: palette surface
   894→    ('BACKGROUND', (0, 3), (-1, 3), colors.white),
   895→    ('GRID', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0.5, TEXT_MUTED),       # Grid: palette muted
   896→    ('VALIGN', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 'MIDDLE'),
   897→    ('LEFTPADDING', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 8),
   898→    ('RIGHTPADDING', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 8),
   899→    ('TOPPADDING', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 6),
   900→    ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 6),
   901→]))
   902→```
   903→
   904→### Table Width Management (⚠️ Prevent Table Overflow)
   905→
   906→**Most common table bug: columns overflow the right page margin because colWidths are hardcoded without checking available space.**
   907→
   908→**Iron Rule: The sum of all colWidths MUST NOT exceed `available_width`.**
   909→
   910→```python
   911→from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
   912→from reportlab.lib.units import inch
   913→
   914→page_width = A4[0]  # 595.28pt
   915→left_margin = 1.0 * inch   # 72pt (adjust to your doc's actual margins)
   916→right_margin = 1.0 * inch  # 72pt
   917→available_width = page_width - left_margin - right_margin  # ≈ 451pt
   918→
   919→# ─── Method 1: Proportional widths (RECOMMENDED) ───
   920→# Define column ratios, auto-scale to fit available_width
   921→col_ratios = [0.25, 0.40, 0.20, 0.15]  # must sum to 1.0
   922→col_widths = [r * available_width for r in col_ratios]
   923→table = Table(data, colWidths=col_widths)
   924→
   925→# ─── Method 2: Fixed + flex columns ───
   926→# Some columns have fixed width (numbers, dates), rest fills remaining space
   927→fixed_cols = {0: 80, 3: 60}  # col 0 = 80pt, col 3 = 60pt
   928→fixed_total = sum(fixed_cols.values())
   929→flex_count = 4 - len(fixed_cols)  # total columns minus fixed
   930→flex_width = (available_width - fixed_total) / flex_count
   931→col_widths = []
   932→for i in range(4):
   933→    col_widths.append(fixed_cols.get(i, flex_width))
   934→table = Table(data, colWidths=col_widths)
   935→
   936→# ─── Method 3: Let ReportLab auto-calculate (simplest, less control) ───
   937→# Pass colWidths=None - ReportLab auto-sizes based on content
   938→# ⚠️ Risk: may still overflow if content is too wide
   939→table = Table(data)  # no colWidths
   940→```
   941→
   942→**Checklist before adding any table:**
   943→1. Calculate `available_width` from your actual page size and margins
   944→2. Sum all colWidths - must be ≤ `available_width`
   945→3. For CJK text: characters are wider than Latin - budget ~12pt per CJK char at 10pt font
   946→4. If a column has long text (descriptions, policies), use Paragraph() wrapping inside cells (not plain strings) - plain strings don't wrap and will overflow
   947→5. Test with the longest expected content in each column
   948→
   949→**Common mistake - plain strings don't wrap:**
   950→```python
   951→# ❌ Plain string: will overflow if text is long
   952→data = [['Policy Name', 'Very long description that keeps going and going']]
   953→# ✅ Paragraph: wraps within column width
   954→from reportlab.platypus import Paragraph
   955→from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet
   956→styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
   957→cell_style = styles['Normal']
   958→data = [[Paragraph('Policy Name', cell_style),
   959→         Paragraph('Very long description that keeps going and going', cell_style)]]
   960→```
   961→
   962→```
   963→Do you need auto-TOC?
   964→├─ YES → Use TocDocTemplate + doc.multiBuild(story)
   965→└─ NO → Use SimpleDocTemplate + doc.build(story)
   966→```
   967→
   968→| Requirement | DocTemplate | Build Method |
   969→|-------------|-------------|--------------|
   970→| Multi-page with TOC | `TocDocTemplate` | `multiBuild()` |
   971→| Single-page or no TOC | `SimpleDocTemplate` | `build()` |
   972→| With Cross-References (no TOC) | `SimpleDocTemplate` | `build()` |
   973→| Both TOC + Cross-References | `TocDocTemplate` | `multiBuild()` |
   974→
   975→**⚠️ CRITICAL**:
   976→- `multiBuild()` is ONLY needed when using `TableOfContents`
   977→- Using `build()` with `TocDocTemplate` = TOC won't work
   978→- Using `multiBuild()` without `TocDocTemplate` = unnecessary overhead
   979→
   980→---
   981→
   982→## Rich Text Formatting
   983→
   984→### Prerequisites
   985→To use `<b>`, `<super>`, `<sub>` tags, you **must**:
   986→1. Register fonts via `registerFont()`
   987→2. Call `registerFontFamily()` to link normal/bold/italic variants
   988→3. Wrap all tagged text in `Paragraph()` objects
   989→
   990→**CRITICAL**: These tags ONLY work inside `Paragraph()` objects. Plain strings will NOT render them.
   991→
   992→### Preventing Unwanted Line Breaks
   993→
   994→```python
   995→# Use non-breaking space to prevent breaking
   996→text = Paragraph("Professors (K.G.\u00A0Palepu) proposed...", style)
   997→
   998→# Use wordWrap='CJK' for proper Chinese typography
   999→styles.add(ParagraphStyle(name='BodyStyle', fontName='Noto Sans SC', fontSize=10.5,
  1000→    leading=18, alignment=TA_LEFT, wordWrap='CJK'))
  1001→
  1002→# Use <br/> for intentional line breaks (NOT \n)
  1003→text = Paragraph("Line 1<br/>Line 2<br/>Line 3", style)
  1004→```
  1005→
  1006→---
  1007→
  1008→## Auto-Generated Table of Contents
  1009→
  1010→### ❌ FORBIDDEN: Manual Table of Contents
  1011→**NEVER manually create TOC with hardcoded page numbers.**
  1012→
  1013→### ⚠️ MANDATORY: TOC requires a cover page
  1014→**Unless the user explicitly requests no cover, if a document has a Table of Contents, it MUST have a cover page.** Structure: Cover (page 1) → TOC (page 2) → Content (page 3+). Do not generate a TOC without a preceding cover page.
  1015→
  1016→### ✅ ALWAYS use auto-generated TOC:
  1017→
  1018→```python
  1019→from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate, Paragraph, PageBreak, Spacer
  1020→from reportlab.platypus.tableofcontents import TableOfContents
  1021→from reportlab.lib.utils import simpleSplit
  1022→import hashlib
  1023→
  1024→class TocDocTemplate(SimpleDocTemplate):
  1025→    def afterFlowable(self, flowable):
  1026→        if hasattr(flowable, 'bookmark_name'):
  1027→            level = getattr(flowable, 'bookmark_level', 0)
  1028→            text = getattr(flowable, 'bookmark_text', '')
  1029→            key = getattr(flowable, 'bookmark_key', '')
  1030→            # MUST pass key as 4th element - without it, TOC entries won't have clickable links
  1031→            self.notify('TOCEntry', (level, text, self.page, key))
  1032→
  1033→doc = TocDocTemplate("document.pdf", pagesize=letter)
  1034→story = []
  1035→
  1036→toc = TableOfContents()
  1037→toc.levelStyles = [
  1038→    ParagraphStyle(name='TOCHeading1', fontSize=14, leftIndent=20, fontName='FreeSerif'),
  1039→    ParagraphStyle(name='TOCHeading2', fontSize=12, leftIndent=40, fontName='FreeSerif'),
  1040→]
  1041→story.append(Paragraph("<b>Table of Contents</b>", styles['Title']))
  1042→story.append(toc)
  1043→story.append(PageBreak())
  1044→
  1045→def add_heading(text, style, level=0):
  1046→    # Generate a unique bookmark key for this heading
  1047→    key = 'h_%s' % hashlib.md5(text.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]
  1048→    p = Paragraph('<a name="%s"/>%s' % (key, text), style)
  1049→    p.bookmark_name = text
  1050→    p.bookmark_level = level
  1051→    p.bookmark_text = text
  1052→    p.bookmark_key = key  # This key enables clickable TOC links
  1053→    return p
  1054→
  1055→# ⚠️ Orphan Heading Prevention (NOT a page break rule)
  1056→# If an H1 heading would appear in the bottom 15% of the page with no room
  1057→# for at least a few lines of content, push it to the next page.
  1058→# This is NOT "every H1 starts a new page" — it only triggers when the heading
  1059→# would be orphaned at the very bottom.
  1060→available_height = A4[1] - top_margin - bottom_margin
  1061→H1_ORPHAN_THRESHOLD = available_height * 0.15  # ~100pt ≈ 3.5cm — enough for heading + 2 lines
  1062→
  1063→def add_major_section(text, style):
  1064→    """Add an H1-level heading with orphan prevention (NOT forced page break)."""
  1065→    return [
  1066→        CondPageBreak(H1_ORPHAN_THRESHOLD),  # Only break if heading would be orphaned at page bottom
  1067→        add_heading(text, style, level=0),
  1068→    ]
  1069→
  1070→story.extend(add_major_section("Chapter 1: Introduction", styles['Heading1']))
  1071→# ... content ...
  1072→
  1073→doc.multiBuild(story)  # MUST use multiBuild for TOC
  1074→```
  1075→
  1076→**⚠️ CRITICAL TOC LINK RULES:**
  1077→1. `afterFlowable` MUST pass `key` as the 4th element in the notify tuple - without it, TOC entries have no clickable links
  1078→2. `add_heading` MUST set `bookmark_key` AND embed `<a name="key"/>` in the Paragraph text - this creates the link destination
  1079→3. The key must be unique per heading - use a hash of the heading text
  1080→
  1081→### TOC with Leader Dots (Copy-Paste Ready)
  1082→
  1083→**⚠️ WARNING**: This manual approach creates a visual-only TOC. It has **NO clickable links** and **NO auto-updated page numbers**. Use the auto-generated TOC above (TocDocTemplate + multiBuild) whenever possible. Only use this leader-dots approach if you need very specific visual styling that the auto TOC cannot provide - and even then, prefer the auto approach.
  1084→
  1085→For a professional TOC with leader dots connecting titles to page numbers:
  1086→
  1087→```python
  1088→from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
  1089→from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate, Table, TableStyle, Paragraph, PageBreak
  1090→from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
  1091→from reportlab.lib import colors
  1092→from reportlab.lib.units import inch
  1093→
  1094→# Setup
  1095→doc = SimpleDocTemplate("report.pdf", pagesize=A4,
  1096→                       leftMargin=0.75*inch, rightMargin=0.75*inch)
  1097→styles = getSampleStyleSheet()
  1098→styles['Heading1'].fontName = 'FreeSerif'
  1099→styles['Heading1'].textColor = colors.black
  1100→
  1101→story = []
  1102→
  1103→# Calculate dimensions
  1104→page_width = A4[0]
  1105→available_width = page_width - 1.5*inch
  1106→page_num_width = 50  # Fixed width for page numbers
  1107→dots_column_width = available_width - 200 - page_num_width
  1108→optimal_dot_count = int(dots_column_width / 4.5)  # ~4.5pt per dot at 7pt font
  1109→
  1110→# Define styles
  1111→toc_style = ParagraphStyle('TOCEntry', parent=styles['Normal'],
  1112→                          fontName='FreeSerif', fontSize=11, leading=16)
  1113→dots_style = ParagraphStyle('LeaderDots', parent=styles['Normal'],
  1114→                           fontName='FreeSerif', fontSize=7, leading=16)
  1115→
  1116→# Build TOC
  1117→toc_data = [
  1118→    [Paragraph('<b>Table of Contents</b>', styles['Heading1']), '', ''],
  1119→    ['', '', ''],
  1120→]
  1121→
  1122→entries = [('Section 1', '5'), ('Section 2', '10')]
  1123→for title, page in entries:
  1124→    toc_data.append([
  1125→        Paragraph(title, toc_style),
  1126→        Paragraph('.' * optimal_dot_count, dots_style),
  1127→        Paragraph(page, toc_style)
  1128→    ])
  1129→
  1130→toc_table = Table(toc_data, colWidths=[None, dots_column_width, page_num_width])
  1131→toc_table.setStyle(TableStyle([
  1132→    ('GRID', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0, colors.white),
  1133→    ('LINEBELOW', (0, 0), (0, 0), 1.5, colors.black),
  1134→    ('ALIGN', (0, 0), (0, -1), 'LEFT'),
  1135→    ('ALIGN', (1, 0), (1, -1), 'LEFT'),
  1136→    ('ALIGN', (2, 0), (2, -1), 'RIGHT'),
  1137→    ('VALIGN', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 'TOP'),
  1138→    ('LEFTPADDING', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
  1139→    ('RIGHTPADDING', (0, 0), (-1, -1), 0),
  1140→    ('TOPPADDING', (0, 2), (-1, -1), 3),
  1141→    ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0, 2), (-1, -1), 3),
  1142→    ('TEXTCOLOR', (1, 2), (1, -1), TEXT_MUTED),  # From palette --c-muted
  1143→]))
  1144→
  1145→story.append(toc_table)
  1146→story.append(PageBreak())
  1147→doc.build(story)
  1148→```
  1149→
  1150→**MANDATORY Leader Dots Rules:**
  1151→
  1152→| Rule | Requirement |
  1153→|------|-------------|
  1154→| Column widths | ✅ MUST use fixed values. Percentage-based widths are **STRICTLY FORBIDDEN**. |
  1155→| Dot count | ✅ MUST calculate dynamically: `int(dots_column_width / 4.5)`. Hard-coded counts are **STRICTLY FORBIDDEN**. |
  1156→| Page number column | ✅ MUST be at least 40pt wide. |
  1157→| Dot font size | ✅ MUST NOT exceed 8pt. |
  1158→| Dot alignment | ✅ MUST be LEFT-aligned (visual flow from title). |
  1159→| Padding | ✅ MUST be exactly 0 between columns. |
  1160→| Bold text | ✅ MUST use `Paragraph('<b>Text</b>', style)`. Plain strings like `'<b>Text</b>'` are **STRICTLY FORBIDDEN**. |
  1161→| Indentation | Use leading spaces for hierarchy (e.g., `"    1.1 Subsection"`). |
  1162→
  1163→### TOC Post-Generation Validation (MANDATORY)
  1164→
  1165→After generating any PDF with a Table of Contents, run:
  1166→
  1167→```bash
  1168→python3 "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/toc_validate.py" check-pdf output.pdf
  1169→```
  1170→
  1171→If any errors are returned, fix the code and regenerate. Common issues:
  1172→- `TOC_ALL_SAME_PAGE` → You used `build()` instead of `multiBuild()`, so all page numbers are stuck at 1.
  1173→- `TOC_NO_ENTRIES` → Headings are missing `bookmark_name`/`bookmark_level` attributes.
  1174→- `TOC_PAGES_INVALID` → A TOC entry references a page beyond the document's total page count.
  1175→

  1177→
  1178→## Cross-References (Figures, Tables, Bibliography)
  1179→
  1180→Pre-register all figures, tables, and references BEFORE using them in text.
  1181→
  1182→```python
  1183→class CrossReferenceDocument:
  1184→    def __init__(self):
  1185→        self.figures = {}
  1186→        self.tables = {}
  1187→        self.refs = {}
  1188→        self.figure_counter = 0
  1189→        self.table_counter = 0
  1190→        self.ref_counter = 0
  1191→
  1192→    def add_figure(self, name):
  1193→        if name not in self.figures:
  1194→            self.figure_counter += 1
  1195→            self.figures[name] = self.figure_counter
  1196→        return self.figures[name]
  1197→
  1198→    def add_table(self, name):
  1199→        if name not in self.tables:
  1200→            self.table_counter += 1
  1201→            self.tables[name] = self.table_counter
  1202→        return self.tables[name]
  1203→
  1204→    def add_reference(self, name):
  1205→        if name not in self.refs:
  1206→            self.ref_counter += 1
  1207→            self.refs[name] = self.ref_counter
  1208→        return self.refs[name]
  1209→```
  1210→
  1211→---
  1212→
  1213→## Image Handling
  1214→
  1215→### Diagram Embedding Rules (MANDATORY)
  1216→
  1217→**Figures are block-level Flowables.** Every image/diagram in ReportLab MUST be appended to `story` as a standalone `Image()` or wrapped in `KeepTogether([image, caption])`. Never simulate floating layouts by placing images inside Paragraph text or multi-column Table cells alongside body text.
  1218→
  1219→**Complex diagrams (>12 nodes)**: Decompose into a ReportLab Table (details) + a simplified diagram image (overview). The diagram gives the big picture; the table carries precision. See SKILL.md "Complex Diagram Strategy".
  1220→
  1221→**Diagram quality**: When generating flowcharts/diagrams for PDF embedding (via Playwright screenshot or ReportLab Drawing), follow the diagram content quality rules in SKILL.md § "Diagram Content Quality Rules". Key points: connectors must not pass through nodes, no high-saturation large fills, multi-arrow convergence must use merge pattern, font sizes must be legible at final embedded size (see context-specific minimums in SKILL.md).
  1222→
  1223→**Cross-brief diagram pipeline**: For flowcharts/architecture diagrams, generate via **Playwright+CSS** (HTML nodes + CSS layout + SVG connectors), screenshot at 2× device scale factor for 300dpi print quality, then embed with `Image()`. See SKILL.md § "Diagram Generation Strategy" for the full pipeline.
  1224→
  1225→**🚫 FORBIDDEN: TikZ standalone → PNG pipeline in Report brief.** Report uses ReportLab, not LaTeX. Going through TikZ requires a LaTeX compiler, adds compilation steps, and models frequently produce broken TikZ code. Use Playwright+CSS instead - it's native to the existing cover rendering engine.
  1226→
  1227→```python
  1228→# Playwright+CSS diagram → PNG → ReportLab embedding
  1229→# 1. LLM generates diagram.html (CSS grid/flexbox nodes + SVG arrows)
  1230→# 2. Playwright screenshots at 2× scale (300dpi equivalent)
  1231→# 3. Embed:
  1232→from reportlab.platypus import Image
  1233→img = Image('diagram.png', width=450)  # auto height via aspect ratio
  1234→story.append(img)                       # block-level Flowable
  1235→```
  1236→
  1237→**⚠️ CRITICAL: Preserve aspect ratio - NEVER hardcode both width and height without reading actual image dimensions.**
  1238→
  1239→**⚠️ CRITICAL: Always constrain to available space - NEVER insert at original size if it exceeds container width/height.**
  1240→
  1241→Pie charts become ellipses, radar charts become diamonds, photos get stretched if you set arbitrary width/height.
  1242→
  1243→**→ MUST READ: `typesetting/overflow.md`** — read the complete bounding box system before writing layout code.
  1244→
  1245→```python
  1246→from PIL import Image as PILImage
  1247→from reportlab.platypus import Image
  1248→from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
  1249→from reportlab.lib.units import inch
  1250→
  1251→def embed_image(path: str, max_width: float = None, max_height: float = None) -> Image:
  1252→    """Embed image with preserved aspect ratio, constrained to container.
  1253→
  1254→    ALWAYS use this pattern. Never hardcode both width and height.
  1255→    If max_width is None, defaults to available_width (page - margins).
  1256→    """
  1257→    if max_width is None:
  1258→        max_width = A4[0] - 2.0 * inch  # Default: A4 width minus 1" margins each side
  1259→    if max_height is None:
  1260→        max_height = A4[1] * 0.35  # ~294pt ≈ 10cm — leaves room for caption + surrounding text on same page
  1261→
  1262→    pil_img = PILImage.open(path)
  1263→    orig_w, orig_h = pil_img.size
  1264→
  1265→    # Scale to fit within BOTH max_width and max_height
  1266→    ratio_w = max_width / orig_w if orig_w > max_width else 1.0
  1267→    ratio_h = max_height / orig_h if orig_h > max_height else 1.0
  1268→    ratio = min(ratio_w, ratio_h)
  1269→
  1270→    return Image(path, width=orig_w * ratio, height=orig_h * ratio)
  1271→
  1272→# Usage:
  1273→available_width = A4[0] - left_margin - right_margin
  1274→img = embed_image('chart.png', max_width=available_width, max_height=300)
  1275→story.append(img)
  1276→```
  1277→
  1278→```python
  1279→# ❌ NEVER do this - distorts the image:
  1280→img = Image('chart.png', width=400, height=250)  # arbitrary height breaks aspect ratio
  1281→```
  1282→

  1315→## MANDATORY: Post-Generation Code Sanitization
  1316→
  1317→**After writing PDF generation code and BEFORE executing it**, sanitize using:
  1318→
  1319→```bash
  1320→# Step 0: Set skill root path (see SKILL.md § Script Path Setup)
  1321→PDF_SKILL_DIR="<skill_directory>"
  1322→
  1323→# Step 1: Write code to .py file
  1324→cat > generate_pdf.py << 'PYEOF'
  1325→# ... your PDF generation code ...
  1326→PYEOF
  1327→
  1328→# Step 2: Sanitize (MUST run before execution)
  1329→python3 "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/pdf.py" code.sanitize generate_pdf.py
  1330→
  1331→# Step 3: Execute
  1332→python3 generate_pdf.py
  1333→
  1334→# Step 4: Add metadata (MUST run after PDF creation)
  1335→python3 "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/pdf.py" meta.brand output.pdf
  1336→
  1337→# Step 5: Check for missing glyphs (RECOMMENDED)
  1338→python3 "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/pdf.py" font.check output.pdf
  1339→
  1340→# Step 6: Check TOC quality (if document has TOC)
  1341→python3 "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/pdf.py" toc.check output.pdf
  1342→
  1343→# Step 7: PDF quality assurance scan (MUST run after all other checks)
  1344→python3 "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/pdf_qa.py" output.pdf
  1345→```
  1346→
  1347→**FORBIDDEN patterns:**
  1348→```bash
  1349→# ❌ PROHIBITED: python -c with inline code
  1350→python -c "from reportlab... doc.build(story)"
  1351→
  1352→# ❌ PROHIBITED: heredoc without saving to file first
  1353→python3 << 'EOF'
  1354→from reportlab...
  1355→EOF
  1356→
  1357→# ❌ PROHIBITED: executing .py without sanitizing
  1358→python generate_pdf.py  # Missing sanitization!
  1359→```

  1366→
  1367→```python
  1368→from reportlab.platypus import HRFlowable
  1369→from reportlab.lib.colors import red
  1370→
  1371→# Visualize spacing during development - insert between elements
  1372→story.append(table)
  1373→story.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", color=red, thickness=0.5, spaceBefore=0, spaceAfter=0))
  1374→story.append(Spacer(1, 6))
  1375→story.append(HRFlowable(width="100%", color=red, thickness=0.5, spaceBefore=0, spaceAfter=0))
  1376→story.append(caption)
  1377→# Red lines create visual markers to see actual spacing - remove before final build

  1389→
  1390→## Component Reference
  1391→
  1392→### Block types:
  1393→| Type | Description | Notes |
  1394→|------|-------------|-------|
  1395→| h1 | 22pt + accent underline rule | KeepTogether with rule |
  1396→| h2 | 15pt, dark | No rule, no accent |
  1397→| h3 | 11.5pt bold | No accent |
  1398→| body | 10.5pt justified, 17pt leading | Supports `<b>` `<i>` `<font>` |
  1399→| bullet | Body size with `•` prefix | Unordered list |
  1400→| numbered | Body size with N. prefix | Counter auto-resets |
  1401→| callout | Accent left border 4px + light tint bg | Max one per section |
  1402→| table | Accent header + alternating rows + outer box only | Supports fractional col_widths |
  1403→| code | Courier 8.5pt + accent left border | Optional language label |
  1404→| divider | Accent 1.2pt rule | Use sparingly |
  1405→| caption | 8.5pt muted, centered | Below images/tables |
  1406→| chart | matplotlib figure saved as PNG → `Image()` flowable | Generate chart in-script, `fig.savefig()` → embed. Set `plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif']=['Noto Sans SC', 'DejaVu Sans']` for CJK + symbol fallback. Always `tight_layout()`. **Must follow `typesetting/charts.md` rules**: delete top/right spines, dashed grid 20% opacity, donut default for pie, anti-stacking labels. |
  1407→| quote | Body italic + left indent 24pt + muted accent left border 2px | For blockquotes / testimonials |
  1408→| bibliography | Hanging indent (firstLineIndent=-24pt, leftIndent=24pt) | GB/T 7714 or APA format per language |
  1409→| math | Rendered via `<super>` `<sub>` tags in Paragraph | For inline math; complex equations → use academic brief instead |
  1410→
  1411→### Header / footer:
  1412→- Header: document title (left, 7.5pt, muted) + accent rule (1.5pt, full width)
  1413→- Footer: author (left, 7.5pt, muted) + page number (right, 7.5pt, muted) + light rule above
  1414→
  1415→### Custom flowables (preferred over Table hacks):
  1416→- **CalloutBox**: accent 4px left border + light tint background - cleaner than Table simulation
  1417→- **BibliographyItem**: hanging indent reference entry
  1418→

  1433→## Document-Type Quick Reference
  1434→
  1435→### 📋 Invoice / Receipt
  1436→
  1437→Key elements:
  1438→- Company logo/name at top
  1439→- Invoice number + date (right-aligned)
  1440→- Seller/buyer info block (2-column layout)
  1441→- Line items table: Item, Quantity, Unit Price, Amount
  1442→- Total row with bold font
  1443→- Tax info + payment terms at bottom
  1444→- Stamp/seal placeholder (empty circle or rect with "Seal Here" text)
  1445→
  1446→```python
  1447→# Invoice table pattern
  1448→data = [['Item', 'Qty', 'Unit Price', 'Amount']]
  1449→data += [[item['name'], item['qty'], item['price'], item['total']] for item in items]
  1450→data.append(['', '', 'Total:', f'¥{total}'])
  1451→```
  1452→
  1453→### 📝 Contract / Legal Document
  1454→
  1455→Key elements:
  1456→- Title centered, bold, 18pt
  1457→- Numbered clauses with auto-increment
  1458→- Signature block at bottom: Party A / Party B with date line
  1459→- Page numbers mandatory
  1460→
  1461→```python
  1462→# Signature block pattern
  1463→sig_data = [
  1464→    ['Party A (Seal): ________________', 'Party B (Seal): ________________'],
  1465→    ['Date: ____/____/________', 'Date: ____/____/________'],
  1466→]
  1467→sig_table = Table(sig_data, colWidths=[250, 250])
  1468→```
  1469→
  1470→### 📊 Book / Long Document
  1471→
  1472→For documents with 10+ pages:
  1473→1. Generate TOC with `TableOfContents()` from reportlab.platypus
  1474→2. Use `CondPageBreak(H1_ORPHAN_THRESHOLD)` before H1 headings (NOT `PageBreak()` — never force page breaks between chapters)
  1475→3. Add running headers/footers with chapter title + page number
  1476→4. Run `python3 "$PDF_SKILL_DIR/scripts/toc_validate.py" output.pdf` to verify TOC links
  1477→5. Consider using `bookmarks=True` for PDF outline navigation
  1478→
  1479→---
  1480→
  1481→### 📝 Exam / Quiz / Test Paper / Worksheet
  1482→
  1483→**Exam papers have unique layout requirements. These rules are MANDATORY when generating any test/quiz/exam document.**
  1484→
  1485→#### Numbering & Structure
  1486→```
  1487→一、选择题（每小题 3 分，共 30 分）          ← Section header (宋体/黑体 14pt Bold)
  1488→
  1489→1. 以下哪个不是 Python 内置数据类型？     ← Question stem (12pt)
  1490→   A. int                                   ← Options: indented 24pt (2em)
  1491→   B. float                                 ← Each option on new line or 2×2 grid
  1492→   C. array
  1493→   D. str
  1494→
  1495→2. 以下表达式的值为？                     ← Next question
  1496→   A. True    B. False                      ← Short options: inline 2×2 grid OK
  1497→   C. None    D. Error
  1498→```
  1499→
  1500→#### Option Indentation (MANDATORY)
  1501→```python
  1502→# ReportLab styles for exam papers
  1503→question_style = ParagraphStyle(
  1504→    'Question', fontSize=12, leading=16,
  1505→    leftIndent=0, firstLineIndent=0,
  1506→    spaceBefore=12, spaceAfter=4,
  1507→)
  1508→option_style = ParagraphStyle(
  1509→    'Option', fontSize=12, leading=16,
  1510→    leftIndent=24,   # ← MANDATORY: 24pt indent from question stem
  1511→    firstLineIndent=0,
  1512→    spaceBefore=2, spaceAfter=2,
  1513→)
  1514→```
  1515→
  1516→#### Option Layout Decision
  1517→```python
  1518→def format_options(options):
  1519→    """
  1520→    Decide option layout based on content length.
  1521→    Short options (≤4 chars each, ≤4 options) → 2×2 grid table
  1522→    Long options or >4 options → vertical list, one per line
  1523→    """
  1524→    max_len = max(len(opt) for opt in options)
  1525→    if len(options) <= 4 and max_len <= 6:  # CJK: 4 chars; Latin: 6 chars
  1526→        # 2×2 grid using Table
  1527→        grid = Table(
  1528→            [[options[0], options[1]], [options[2], options[3]]],
  1529→            colWidths=[200, 200], hAlign='LEFT'
  1530→        )
  1531→        grid.setStyle(TableStyle([
  1532→            ('LEFTPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 24),  # Match option indent
  1533→            ('VALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'TOP'),
  1534→        ]))
  1535→        return grid
  1536→    else:
  1537→        # Vertical list
  1538→        return [Paragraph(f'{opt}', option_style) for opt in options]
  1539→```
  1540→
  1541→#### Answer Space Reservation (MANDATORY)
  1542→
  1543→**Every question MUST have adequate answer space. No exceptions.**
  1544→
  1545→| Question Type | Minimum Space | Implementation |
  1546→|--------------|---------------|----------------|
  1547→| Multiple choice | 0 extra (options are the answer) | Just question + options |
  1548→| Fill-in-the-blank | Inline underline, min 80pt width | `<u>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</u>` or ReportLab `HRFlowable` |
  1549→| Short answer | 40-60pt (2-3 lines) | `Spacer(1, 50)` |
  1550→| Calculation / math work | 120-200pt (6-10 lines) | `Spacer(1, 160)` with light guide lines |
  1551→| Essay / long answer | 200-400pt (10-20 lines) | `Spacer(1, 300)` with light guide lines |
  1552→| Proof / derivation | 160-250pt (8-12 lines) | `Spacer(1, 200)` |
  1553→
  1554→```python
  1555→# Answer line helper — light dashed lines for handwriting
  1556→def add_answer_lines(story, num_lines=8, line_spacing=20):
  1557→    """Add light guide lines for handwritten answers."""
  1558→    for i in range(num_lines):
  1559→        story.append(Spacer(1, line_spacing))
  1560→        story.append(HRFlowable(
  1561→            width='90%', thickness=0.3,
  1562→            color=colors.Color(0.8, 0.8, 0.8),  # Light gray
  1563→            dash=[4, 4],  # Dashed
  1564→            spaceAfter=0, spaceBefore=0
  1565→        ))
  1566→```
  1567→
  1568→#### Page Density
  1569→- `spaceBefore=12pt` between questions minimum
  1570→- `spaceBefore=24pt` before section headers (一、二、三)
  1571→- Score indicator after question number: `1. (分值: 5分)` or `1. [5 pts]`
  1572→- Page header: exam title + time limit + total score
  1573→- No cover page unless explicitly requested
  1574→
  1575→---
  1576→
  1577→## Data-to-Ink Ratio Rules (MANDATORY for Reports)
  1578→
  1579→**CRITICAL: Do NOT write long paragraphs to describe data trends.** You MUST extract metrics and trends into structured visual elements.
  1580→
  1581→### Pattern Detection Table
  1582→
  1583→Before writing ANY body paragraph, scan the text. If you find any of these patterns, extract them:
  1584→
  1585→| Raw text pattern | ❌ FORBIDDEN | ✅ REQUIRED visual form |
  1586→|-----------------|-------------|----------------------|
  1587→| "revenue grew 12% to $4.2M" | Bury in paragraph prose | CalloutBox with bold `+12%` + `$4.2M` |
  1588→| "latency dropped from 120ms to 75ms" | Long explanatory sentence | CalloutBox or metrics table row |
  1589→| "Q1→Q2→Q3: 10%→25%→40%" | Inline numbers in text | Chart (matplotlib PNG → `Image()`) |
  1590→| "first...second...third..." steps | Paragraph with ordinal words | Numbered Table or process list |
  1591→| "compared to last year / vs Q2" | Nested comparisons in prose | Side-by-side comparison table |
  1592→| "accounted for 60% of total" | Percentage in paragraph | Pie chart or stacked bar chart |
  1593→
  1594→### ReportLab CalloutBox Template
  1595→
  1596→Use this pattern for extracted metrics - it's visually clean and takes only 5 lines:
  1597→
  1598→```python
  1599→stat_style = ParagraphStyle(
  1600→    name='StatBig', fontName='FreeSerif', fontSize=22,
  1601→    leading=26, textColor=ACCENT, alignment=TA_CENTER  # From palette
  1602→)
  1603→label_style = ParagraphStyle(
  1604→    name='StatLabel', fontName='FreeSerif', fontSize=9,
  1605→    leading=12, textColor=TEXT_MUTED, alignment=TA_CENTER  # From palette
  1606→)
  1607→
  1608→callout = Table(
  1609→    [[Paragraph('<b>+12%</b>', stat_style)],
  1610→     [Paragraph('Revenue Growth vs Q2', label_style)]],
  1611→    colWidths=[160]
  1612→)
  1613→callout.setStyle(TableStyle([
  1614→    ('BACKGROUND', (0,0), (-1,-1), BG_SURFACE),      # From palette --c-mid
  1615→    ('BOX', (0,0), (-1,-1), 1, ACCENT),               # From palette --c-accent
  1616→    ('TOPPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 10),
  1617→    ('BOTTOMPADDING', (0,0), (-1,-1), 10),
  1618→    ('VALIGN', (0,0), (-1,-1), 'MIDDLE'),
  1619→]))
  1620→story.append(KeepTogether(callout))
  1621→```
  1622→
  1623→---
  1624→
  1625→## Final Checklist (Mandatory before delivery)
  1626→
  1627→### Code & Build
  1628→
  1629→- [ ] **Font restriction**: Only 6 registered fonts used
  1630→- [ ] **Font family registered**: `registerFontFamily()` called for all used fonts
  1631→- [ ] **⚠️ MANDATORY font fallback**: `install_font_fallback()` called after font registration — **#1 cause of Helvetica garbled text (乱码)**
  1632→- [ ] **Rich text tags**: Only inside `Paragraph()` objects
  1633→- [ ] **Table cells**: ALL text wrapped in `Paragraph()` — no plain strings
  1634→- [ ] **Scientific notation**: Large/small numbers use `<super>` tags
  1635→- [ ] **Chinese text**: `wordWrap='CJK'` in ParagraphStyle
  1636→- [ ] **Line breaks**: Using `<br/>` not `\n`
  1637→- [ ] **Headings**: Bold with `<b>` tags
  1638→- [ ] **Sanitization**: Code sanitized before execution
  1639→
  1640→### Pagination & Overflow
  1641→
  1642→- [ ] **Last page fill ratio ≥ 40%**: No large blank areas on final page
  1643→- [ ] **Major section 3/4 threshold**: H1 headings must NOT start in bottom 25% of page. Use `CondPageBreak(available_height * 0.25)`
  1644→- [ ] **Tables don't split**: Small tables `break-inside: avoid`. Large tables `thead { display: table-header-group }`
  1645→- [ ] **No orphan headings**: Use `break-after: avoid` / KeepTogether
  1646→- [ ] **sum(colWidths) ≤ available_width**: Verified in code
  1647→- [ ] **Images/charts scaled**: Always `fit_image()` or `max-width: 100%`
  1648→- [ ] **Long tables repeatRows=1**
  1649→- [ ] **CJK wordWrap='CJK'**
  1650→- [ ] **URLs word-break**: `overflow-wrap: break-word`
  1651→
  1652→### Color & Design
  1653→
  1654→- [ ] **Entire document ≤ 5 colors**: Primary + secondary + accent + neutral + background
  1655→- [ ] **All colors from palette**: Secondary/accent derived via lightness/saturation shift
  1656→- [ ] **Table headers use `HEADER_FILL`** (M tier, S ≤ 0.30) — NOT `ACCENT`
  1657→- [ ] **Table stripes use `TABLE_STRIPE`** (L tier, S ≤ 0.15)
  1658→- [ ] **Decorative elements ≤ 3 per page** (cover exempt)
  1659→- [ ] **No rainbow/multi-color schemes**: Single-family palette only
  1660→- [ ] **No decorative borders/textures on body pages**
  1661→- [ ] **2-typeface maximum**
  1662→- [ ] **🚫 NO stock images / clipart / AI decorations**
  1663→
  1664→### Cover
  1665→
  1666→- [ ] **正文编号从 1 开始**: 封面/目录不是章节，Step 3.5 映射表已输出
  1667→- [ ] **Cover default ON** for reports/proposals/analysis ≥ 3 pages
  1668→- [ ] **Single PDF output**: Cover merged as page 0 via pypdf
  1669→- [ ] **Page isolation**: Cover never shares page with TOC/body
  1670→- [ ] **🚫 NEVER use ReportLab for covers** — HTML/Playwright only
  1671→- [ ] **Z-Index Layers**: Layer 0 (bg) → 1 (decorative, CLIPPED) → 2 (lines) → 3 (text)
  1672→- [ ] **No dark/gradient backgrounds**
  1673→- [ ] **Cover whitespace ≥ 60%**
  1674→- [ ] **Cover colors from palette.cascade** `:root` CSS variables
  1675→
  1676→### Tables
  1677→
  1678→- [ ] **ALL tables horizontally centered** (`hAlign='CENTER'`)
  1679→- [ ] **Table width 85-100%** of content area
  1680→- [ ] **Table headers**: White bold text on `HEADER_FILL`
  1681→
  1682→### Charts
  1683→
  1684→- [ ] **No text overlap**: Labels, values, legends collision-free
  1685→- [ ] **Chart-to-text separation**: 24pt above/below, 8pt to caption
  1686→- [ ] **Legend outside chart area**: `bbox_to_anchor`
  1687→- [ ] **Pie → Donut** by default (hole_ratio 60-70%)
  1688→- [ ] **Axis cleanup**: Top/right spines deleted
  1689→
  1690→### Layout
  1691→
  1692→- [ ] **Margin symmetry**: `left_margin == right_margin`
  1693→- [ ] **Full-bleed (Playwright)**: `@page { margin: 0 }`
  1694→- [ ] **Content centering**: No left/right drift
  1695→- [ ] **Anti-void edges**: Content fills ≥ 60%
  1696→
  1697→### Post-Build
  1698→
  1699→- [ ] **Metadata**: `pdf.py meta.brand` — Title matches filename, Author/Creator = "Z.ai"
  1700→- [ ] **Blank page cleanup**: `pdf.py pages.clean`
  1701→- [ ] **Glyph check**: `pdf.py font.check`
  1702→- [ ] **TOC check**: `pdf.py toc.check` (if document has TOC)
  1703→- [ ] **PDF QA scan**: `pdf_qa.py`
